```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

The method uses:
- FFT-based fast evaluation of a*a and of subgradient-like sensitivities.
- Exact quadratic line searches for two structured moves:
    1) Frank-Wolfe-style move toward a coordinate: a -> (1-t)a + t e_j
    2) Pair-transfer move: move mass from q to j
- Soft active-set weighting of the largest convolution coefficients.
- Symmetrization and multiplicative perturbations for escape.
- Length exploration and repeated parameter sweeps.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def candidate_key(item):
    """Sort key for candidate moves."""
    return item[0]


def norm_sum1(a):
    """Clamp non-negative and normalize sum to one."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def conv_square(a):
    """Full convolution a*a using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fft_size = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_size)[:size]
    return np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)


def safe_score(a):
    """Call the external evaluator safely."""
    try:
        lst = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)]
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return float("inf")


def estimate_score(a):
    """Internal objective estimate assuming sum(a)=1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.maximum(conv_square(a), 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    return 2.0 * float(a.size) * m


def soft_weights(c, tau, K):
    """
    Return probability weights over the top K coefficients of c.
    tau is a temperature. K top values are used; tau=0 gives equal weights top K.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([0.0]), np.array([], dtype=int), np.array([0.0])
    try:
        K = int(K)
    except Exception:
        K = min(m, 128)
    if K < 1:
        K = 1
    if K > m:
        K = m

    if tau <= 0:
        if K >= m:
            w = np.ones(m) / float(m)
            return w, np.arange(m), w.copy()
        idx = np.argpartition(c, m - K)[-(K):]
        w = np.zeros(m)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
        return w, idx, w[idx].copy()

    if K >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
        vals = c
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, m - K)[-(K):]
        vals = c[idx]

    vmax = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(vmax):
        vmax = 0.0
    scaled = np.exp(tau * (vals - vmax))
    s = float(np.sum(scaled))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        scaled = np.ones_like(vals) / float(vals.size)

    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = scaled
    return w, idx, scaled


def gradient_from_top(top_idx, top_vals, a):
    """
    Fast sparse gradient-like sensitivity:
        s_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}
    where (w_k) are nonzero top coefficients.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(top_idx) == 0:
        return s
    mask = top_vals > 1e-300
    idx = np.asarray(top_idx[mask], dtype=int)
    vals = np.asarray(top_vals[mask], dtype=np.float64)
    for t in range(len(idx)):
        k = int(idx[t])
        w = float(vals[t])
        if w == 0.0:
            continue
        i0 = k - n + 1
        if i0 < 0:
            i0 = 0
        i1 = k + 1
        if i1 > n:
            i1 = n
        if i0 < i1:
            start = k - i1 + 1
            stop = k - i0 + 1
            seg = a[start:stop][::-1]
            s[i0:i1] += w * seg
    return s


def gradient_from_weights_fft(p, a):
    """
    Same sensitivity using FFT, suitable for dense weights.
        s_i = sum_j a_j p_{i+j}
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = p.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 1 << (n + m - 2).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, L)
    out_idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n)
    return np.asarray(conv[out_idx], dtype=np.float64)


def gen_candidate_ts(lin, quad, tmax):
    """Generate candidate step sizes for a quadratic approximation."""
    if not np.isfinite(tmax) or tmax <= 1e-15:
        return [0.0]
    ts = [0.0, float(tmax)]
    if np.isfinite(lin) and np.isfinite(quad):
        if abs(quad) > 1e-14:
            t0 = -lin / (2.0 * quad)
            if np.isfinite(t0):
                if t0 > 1e-15:
                    ts.append(float(t0))
                    ts.append(float(0.5 * t0))
                    ts.append(float(1.5 * t0))
        else:
            if lin < 0.0:
                ts.append(float(tmax))
            else:
                ts.append(0.0)
    for f in (0.01, 0.03, 0.10, 0.30, 0.60, 0.90):
        t = f * tmax
        if 1e-15 < t < tmax - 1e-15:
            ts.append(float(t))

    arr = np.array(ts, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr[(arr >= -1e-15) & (arr <= tmax + 1e-15)]
    if arr.size == 0:
        return [0.0]
    np.clip(arr, 0.0, tmax, out=arr)
    arr = np.unique(arr)
    if arr.size > 12:
        keep = [0.0, tmax]
        for f in (0.01, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 0.80):
            keep.append(f * tmax)
        arr = np.unique(np.array(keep, dtype=np.float64))
    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def min_scalar_quad(W, lin, quad, ts):
    """Minimum of W + lin*t + quad*t^2 over candidate ts."""
    best = float("inf")
    for t in ts:
        val = W + lin * t + quad * t * t
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best:
            best = val
    return best


def eval_fw_move(c, a, j, ts, cur_m, tmax=1.0):
    """
    Evaluate max(a*a) for FW move a_t = a + t(e_j - a).
    Returns best t and resulting max coefficient.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = c.size
    best_t = 0.0
    best_val = cur_m

    S = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 0:
        S[j:j + n] = a
    L = 2.0 * S - 2.0 * c
    B = c - 2.0 * S
    idx2 = 2 * j
    if 0 <= idx2 < m:
        B[idx2] += 1.0

    for t in ts:
        tt = float(t)
        if tt < 0.0:
            tt = 0.0
        if tt > tmax:
            tt = tmax
        if tt <= 1e-15:
            val = cur_m
        else:
            arr = c + tt * L + tt * tt * B
            val = float(np.max(arr))
        if val < best_val - 1e-17:
            best_val = val
            best_t = tt

    if 0.0 < best_t < tmax:
        step = max(1e-6, 0.05 * best_t)
        step = min(step, tmax / 3.0)
        for _ in range(3):
            if step <= 1e-14:
                break
            improved = False
            for f in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0):
                t = best_t + f * step
                if t < 0.0:
                    t = 0.0
                if t > tmax:
                    t = tmax
                if t <= 1e-15:
                    val = cur_m
                else:
                    val = float(np.max(c + t * L + t * t * B))
                if val < best_val - 1e-17:
                    best_val = val
                    best_t = t
                    improved = True
            if not improved:
                break
            step *= 0.4

    return best_t, best_val


def eval_pair_move(c, a, q, j, ts, cur_m, tmax):
    """
    Evaluate max(a*a) for moving mass from q to j.
    a_t = a + t(e_j - e_q), 0 <= t <= a_q.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = c.size
    if q == j or tmax <= 1e-15:
        return 0.0, cur_m

    best_t = 0.0
    best_val = cur_m

    L = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    L[j:j + n] += 2.0 * a
    L[q:q + n] -= 2.0 * a
    mods = ((2 * j, 1.0), (2 * q, 1.0), (j + q, -2.0))

    for t in ts:
        tt = float(t)
        if tt < 0.0:
            tt = 0.0
        if tt > tmax:
            tt = tmax
        if tt <= 1e-15:
            val = cur_m
        else:
            arr = c + tt * L
            tt2 = tt * tt
            for idx, coef in mods:
                if 0 <= idx < m:
                    arr[idx] += coef * tt2
            val = float(np.max(arr))
        if val < best_val - 1e-17:
            best_val = val
            best_t = tt

    if 0.0 < best_t < tmax:
        step = max(1e-8, 0.05 * best_t)
        step = min(step, 0.1 * tmax)
        for _ in range(3):
            if step <= 1e-14:
                break
            improved = False
            for f in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0):
                t = best_t + f * step
                if t < 0.0:
                    t = 0.0
                if t > tmax:
                    t = tmax
                if t <= 1e-15:
                    val = cur_m
                else:
                    arr = c + t * L
                    tt2 = t * t
                    for idx, coef in mods:
                        if 0 <= idx < m:
                            arr[idx] += coef * tt2
                    val = float(np.max(arr))
                if val < best_val - 1e-17:
                    best_val = val
                    best_t = t
                    improved = True
            if not improved:
                break
            step *= 0.4

    return best_t, best_val


def apply_fw_move(a, c, j, t):
    """Apply FW move exactly and update a, c, max."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = c.size
    t = float(t)
    if t < 0.0:
        t = 0.0
    if t > 1.0:
        t = 1.0
    if t <= 1e-15:
        return a, c, float(np.max(c))

    S = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    S[j:j + n] = a
    L = 2.0 * S - 2.0 * c
    B = c - 2.0 * S
    idx2 = 2 * j
    if 0 <= idx2 < m:
        B[idx2] += 1.0
    t2 = t * t

    c = c + t * L + t2 * B
    a = a * (1.0 - t)
    a[j] += t
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    sa = float(a.sum())
    if sa < 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        c = conv_square(a)
    elif abs(sa - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        a = a / sa
        c = c / (sa * sa)

    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    if cur_m < 0.0:
        cur_m = 0.0
    return a, c, cur_m


def apply_pair_move(a, c, q, j, t):
    """Apply pair transfer q -> j exactly and update a, c, max."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = c.size
    if q == j:
        return a, c, float(np.max(c))

    tmax = float(a[q])
    t = float(t)
    if t < 0.0:
        t = 0.0
    if t > tmax:
        t = tmax
    if t <= 1e-15:
        return a, c, float(np.max(c))

    L = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    L[j:j + n] += 2.0 * a
    L[q:q + n] -= 2.0 * a
    t2 = t * t

    c = c + t * L
    for idx, coef in ((2 * j, 1.0), (2 * q, 1.0), (j + q, -2.0)):
        if 0 <= idx < m:
            c[idx] += coef * t2

    a = a.copy()
    a[j] += t
    a[q] -= t
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    sa = float(a.sum())
    if sa < 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        c = conv_square(a)
    elif abs(sa - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        a = a / sa
        c = c / (sa * sa)

    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    if cur_m < 0.0:
        cur_m = 0.0
    return a, c, cur_m


def try_symmetrize(a, c, cur_m):
    """If 0.5*(a+rev(a)) improves, return improved triple; else None."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return None
    a_sym = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    a_sym = np.maximum(a_sym, 0.0)
    s = float(a_sym.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return None
    a_sym = a_sym / s
    c_sym = np.maximum(conv_square(a_sym), 0.0)
    m_sym = float(np.max(c_sym))
    if m_sym < cur_m - 1e-18:
        return a_sym, c_sym, m_sym
    return None


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, sigma):
    """Multiplicative lognormal perturbation, preserving positivity."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a.copy()
    noise = np.exp(sigma * rng.normal(size=n))
    a_try = a * noise
    a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 0.0)
    s = float(a_try.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return a_try / s


def find_best_move(a, c, s, p, W, cur_m, mode):
    """Generate candidate FW/pair moves and select the best by exact max."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return None
    m = c.size
    s = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(W):
        W = cur_m

    low_cnt = 8 if n > 8 else n
    high_cnt = 8 if n > 8 else n
    low_idx = np.argsort(s)[:low_cnt]
    high_idx = np.argsort(s)[::-1][:high_cnt]

    cand = []

    if mode == "fw" or mode == "mix":
        chars = [int(x) for x in low_idx]
        if 0 not in chars:
            chars.append(0)
        if n - 1 not in chars:
            chars.append(n - 1)
        for j in chars[:10]:
            idx2 = 2 * j
            pidx = float(p[idx2]) if 0 <= idx2 < m else 0.0
            sj = float(s[j])
            lin = 2.0 * (sj - W)
            quad = W - 2.0 * sj + pidx
            ts = gen_candidate_ts(lin, quad, 1.0)
            if len(ts) > 0:
                wmin = min_scalar_quad(W, lin, quad, ts)
                cand.append((wmin, 0, 0, j, ts))

    if mode == "pair" or mode == "mix":
        low_pairs = low_idx[:5]
        high_pairs = high_idx[:5]
        for qq in high_pairs:
            q = int(qq)
            if a[q] <= 1e-15:
                continue
            for jj in low_pairs:
                j = int(jj)
                if j == q:
                    continue
                lin = 2.0 * (float(s[j]) - float(s[q]))
                if lin > 1e-12:
                    continue
                idxj = 2 * j
                idxq = 2 * q
                idxs = j + q
                quad = ((float(p[idxj]) if idxj < m else 0.0) +
                        (float(p[idxq]) if idxq < m else 0.0) -
                        2.0 * (float(p[idxs]) if idxs < m else 0.0))
                tmax = float(a[q])
                if tmax <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                ts = gen_candidate_ts(lin, quad, tmax)
                if len(ts) > 0:
                    wmin = min_scalar_quad(W, lin, quad, ts)
                    cand.append((wmin, 1, q, j, ts))

        # Peak-specific moves: for current top peaks, move one factor to low-partner coordinate.
        top_peak_cnt = 3 if m > 3 else m
        if top_peak_cnt < m:
            kpart = np.argpartition(c, m - top_peak_cnt)[-(top_peak_cnt):]
        else:
            kpart = np.arange(m)

        for kk in kpart:
            k = int(kk)
            i_start = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i_end = min(n, k + 1)
            if i_start >= i_end:
                continue
            ii = np.arange(i_start, i_end)
            vals = a[ii] * a[k - ii]
            maxval = float(np.max(vals))
            if maxval <= 1e-30:
                continue
            q = int(ii[int(np.argmax(vals))])

            partner = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            j_start = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j_end = min(n, k + 1)
            jj = np.arange(j_start, j_end)
            partner[jj] = a[k - jj]

            idx_ord = np.argsort(partner)
            j = int(idx_ord[0])
            if j == q:
                if idx_ord.size > 1:
                    j = int(idx_ord[1])
                else:
                    continue

            linW = 2.0 * (float(s[j]) - float(s[q]))
            idxj = 2 * j
            idxq = 2 * q
            idxs = j + q
            quad = ((float(p[idxj]) if idxj < m else 0.0) +
                    (float(p[idxq]) if idxq < m else 0.0) -
                    2.0 * (float(p[idxs]) if idxs < m else 0.0))
            tmax = float(a[q])
            if tmax <= 1e-15:
                continue
            ts = gen_candidate_ts(linW, quad, tmax)
            if len(ts) > 0:
                wmin = min_scalar_quad(W, linW, quad, ts)
                cand.append((wmin, 1, q, j, ts))

    if len(cand) == 0:
        j = int(np.argmin(s))
        idx2 = 2 * j
        pidx = float(p[idx2]) if 0 <= idx2 < m else 0.0
        lin = 2.0 * (float(s[j]) - W)
        quad = W - 2.0 * float(s[j]) + pidx
        ts = [0.0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50]
        wmin = min_scalar_quad(W, lin, quad, ts)
        cand = [(wmin, 0, 0, j, ts)]

    cand.sort(key=candidate_key)
    eval_top = 12
    limit = len(cand) if len(cand) < eval_top else eval_top

    best_mval = cur_m
    best_info = None

    for ii in range(limit):
        wmin, typ, q, j, ts = cand[ii]
        if typ == 0:
            t_best, m_best = eval_fw_move(c, a, j, ts, cur_m, 1.0)
        else:
            tmax = float(a[q])
            t_best, m_best = eval_pair_move(c, a, q, j, ts, cur_m, tmax)
        if m_best < best_mval - 1e-16:
            best_mval = m_best
            best_info = (typ, q, j, t_best)

    if best_info is None:
        return None
    return (best_info[0], best_info[1], best_info[2], best_info[3], best_mval)


def direct_descent(a_init, time_limit, tau=100.0, K=128, mode="mix",
                   seed=0, early_stop=30.0, verbose=False, recompute_every=3):
    """
    Local direct descent with exact quadratic line searches.
    Returns best sequence (sum normalized) and its max convolution coefficient.
    """
    a = norm_sum1(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        c = np.maximum(conv_square(a), 0.0)
        return a, float(np.max(c))

    c = np.maximum(conv_square(a), 0.0)
    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = cur_m

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    last_improve = t0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    s = None
    p = None
    W = None
    move_count = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        if early_stop > 0 and time.monotonic() - last_improve > early_stop:
            break

        if s is None:
            p, top_idx, top_vals = soft_weights(c, tau, K)
            if K <= 128:
                s = gradient_from_top(top_idx, top_vals, a)
            else:
                s = gradient_from_weights_fft(p, a)
            W = float(np.dot(p, c))
            if not np.isfinite(W):
                W = cur_m

        move = find_best_move(a, c, s, p, W, cur_m, mode)

        if move is not None:
            typ, q, j, t, _new_m = move
            if typ == 0:
                a, c, cur_m = apply_fw_move(a, c, j, t)
            else:
                a, c, cur_m = apply_pair_move(a, c, q, j, t)

            if cur_m < best_m - 1e-18:
                best_m = cur_m
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

            move_count += 1
            if move_count % recompute_every == 0:
                a = norm_sum1(a)
                c = np.maximum(conv_square(a), 0.0)
                cur_m = float(np.max(c))
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-18:
                    best_m = cur_m
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    stall = 0
                s = None

            if stall >= 8:
                a = best_a.copy()
                c = np.maximum(conv_square(a), 0.0)
                cur_m = float(np.max(c))
                best_m = cur_m
                best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0
                s = None
        else:
            escaped = False

            sym = try_symmetrize(a, c, cur_m)
            if sym is not None:
                a, c, cur_m = sym
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-18:
                    best_m = cur_m
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    stall = 0
                escaped = True
                s = None

            if not escaped:
                base_sigma = 0.005 + 0.02 * min(1.0, float(stall) / 20.0)
                for _ in range(4):
                    sigma = base_sigma * (0.5 + rng.random())
                    a_try = perturb_sequence(a, rng, sigma)
                    c_try = np.maximum(conv_square(a_try), 0.0)
                    m_try = float(np.max(c_try))
                    if m_try < cur_m - 1e-15:
                        a = a_try
                        c = c_try
                        cur_m = m_try
                        if m_try < best_m - 1e-18:
                            best_m = m_try
                            best_a = a.copy()
                            last_improve = time.monotonic()
                        stall = 0
                        escaped = True
                        s = None
                        break
                    elif m_try < best_m * 1.02 and rng.random() < 0.3:
                        a = a_try
                        c = c_try
                        cur_m = m_try
                        stall = 0
                        escaped = True
                        s = None
                        break

            if not escaped:
                if cur_m > best_m + 1e-12:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    c = np.maximum(conv_square(a), 0.0)
                    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
                stall += 1
                if stall > 10:
                    break

    if verbose:
        print(f"      direct done: n={n}, est={2.0 * n * best_m:.6f}")
    return best_a, best_m


def make_u_shape(n, alpha):
    """U-shaped endpoint-heavy sequence."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + alpha * (x - 0.5) ** 2
    return norm_sum1(a)


def make_arcsine(n, eps):
    """Endpoint-heavy arcsine-like sequence."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(x * (1.0 - x) + eps)
    return norm_sum1(a)


def make_bimodal(n, width):
    """Mass near both endpoints."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.exp(-x / width) + np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / width) + 0.01
    return norm_sum1(a)


def make_random_smooth(n, seed, modes=30):
    """Positive low-frequency random smooth sequence."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.ones(n) * 0.5
    for f in range(1, modes + 1):
        amp = 0.8 / (f ** 0.3)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        a += amp * rng.normal(0.0, 0.4) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + phase)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-4)
    return norm_sum1(a)


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    """Resample normalized sequence to a new length."""
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    old_n = a.size
    if new_n <= 0:
        return a.copy()
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        b = np.ones(new_n) / float(new_n)
    else:
        b = b / s
    return b


def better_candidate_score(a, current_best):
    """If internal score looks promising, validate with evaluator when possible."""
    sc_est = estimate_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc_est):
        return float("inf")
    if sc_est < current_best - 1e-9:
        sc_true = safe_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc_true):
            return sc_true
        return sc_est
    if sc_est < current_best + 1e-7:
        sc_true = safe_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc_true):
            return sc_true
    return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Main entry point. Runs the optimizer within the given time budget and
    returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    try:
        time_budget = max(60.0, min(1750.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0))
    except Exception:
        time_budget = 1700.0

    # Load previous construction if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
            parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Build initial candidates.
    candidates = []
    if parent is not None:
        ap = norm_sum1(parent)
        candidates.append((ap, "parent"))
        asp = norm_sum1(np.maximum(0.5 * (ap + ap[::-1]), 0.0))
        candidates.append((asp, "parent_sym"))
    n_base = len(parent) if parent is not None else 6200

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_base)
    candidates.append((norm_sum1(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2), "sin2"))
    for alpha in (2.0, 10.0, 50.0, 200.0):
        candidates.append((make_u_shape(n_base, alpha), f"u{alpha}"))
    for eps in (0.05, 0.005, 0.0005):
        candidates.append((make_arcsine(n_base, eps), f"arc{eps}"))
    candidates.append((make_bimodal(n_base, 0.05), "bimodal"))
    candidates.append((make_random_smooth(n_base, 123, 30), "random"))
    candidates.append((make_random_smooth(n_base, 456, 50), "random2"))

    # Select best initial candidate.
    for a, label in candidates:
        if a is None:
            continue
        a = norm_sum1(a)
        sc = better_candidate_score(a, best_score)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a.copy()
            print(f"  initial best [{label}]: {best_score:.6f}, n={len(best_seq)}")

    if best_seq is None:
        n_f = 6200
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_f)
        best_seq = norm_sum1(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2)
        best_score = safe_score(best_seq)
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            best_score = estimate_score(best_seq)
        print(f"  fallback initial: {best_score:.6f}, n={len(best_seq)}")

    current = best_seq.copy()

    # Direct descent schedule. Each tuple: (tau, K, mode, seed, requested_time, early_stop)
    schedule = [
        (150.0, 64, "mix", 10, 80, 35),
        (100.0, 128, "mix", 11, 80, 35),
        (50.0, 256, "mix", 12, 80, 35),
        (1000.0, 32, "mix", 13, 80, 35),
        (0.0, 64, "pair", 14, 80, 30),
        (10.0, 1024, "mix", 15, 80, 40),
        (500.0, 16, "fw", 16, 80, 35),
        (30.0, 128, "mix", 20, 80, 45),
        (150.0, 96, "mix", 30, 80, 45),
        (100.0, 128, "mix", 40, 80, 60),
        (50.0, 256, "mix", 50, 80, 75),
    ]

    for idx, (tau, K, mode, seed, t_req, early) in enumerate(schedule):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
        remaining = time_budget - elapsed
        if remaining < 80:
            break
        t_block = min(float(t_req), max(20.0, remaining * 0.18))
        if t_block < 10:
            continue
        print(f"  direct block {idx}: tau={tau:g}, K={K:g}, mode={mode}, t={t_block:.0f}s")
        try:
            a_out, m_out = direct_descent(
                current, t_block,
                tau=tau, K=int(K), mode=mode, seed=seed,
                early_stop=early, verbose=False, recompute_every=3
            )
            sc = better_candidate_score(a_out, best_score)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a_out.copy()
                current = best_seq.copy()
                print(f"    NEW BEST: {best_score:.6f}, n={len(best_seq)}, t={time.monotonic()-t_global:.0f}s")
            else:
                current = best_seq.copy()
        except Exception:
            current = best_seq.copy()

    # Length exploration.
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 180:
        n_best = len(best_seq)
        factors = [1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 0.8, 1.1, 1.75]
        tried = set()
        for f in factors:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
            remaining = time_budget - elapsed
            if remaining < 120:
                break
            new_n = int(round(n_best * f))
            if new_n in tried:
                continue
            if new_n < 1000 or new_n > 20000:
                continue
            tried.add(new_n)

            a_init = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, new_n)
            a_init = np.maximum(0.5 * (a_init + a_init[::-1]), 0.0)
            a_init = norm_sum1(a_init)

            t_block = min(100.0, remaining * 0.22)
            if t_block < 20:
                break
            print(f"  length n={new_n}, t={t_block:.0f}s")
            try:
                a_out, m_out = direct_descent(a_init, t_block, tau=100.0, K=128,
                                              mode="mix", seed=700 + (new_n % 100),
                                              early_stop=35, verbose=False, recompute_every=3)
                sc = better_candidate_score(a_out, best_score)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = a_out.copy()
                    current = best_seq.copy()
                    print(f"    NEW BEST at length {new_n}: {best_score:.6f}, t={time.monotonic()-t_global:.0f}s")
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final polish on best sequence.
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 40:
        t_block = min(remaining - 20.0, 300.0)
        if t_block > 10:
            print(f"  final polish: t={t_block:.0f}s")
            try:
                a_out, m_out = direct_descent(best_seq, t_block, tau=80.0, K=128,
                                              mode="mix", seed=999, early_stop=90,
                                              verbose=False, recompute_every=3)
                sc = better_candidate_score(a_out, best_score)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = a_out.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Try final symmetrization.
    try:
        a_sym = norm_sum1(np.maximum(0.5 * (best_seq + best_seq[::-1]), 0.0))
        sc_sym = better_candidate_score(a_sym, best_score)
        if np.isfinite(sc_sym) and sc_sym < best_score:
            best_score = sc_sym
            best_seq = a_sym.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Final validation and cleanup.
    final_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    final_seq = np.clip(final_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    fs = safe_score(final_seq)
    if np.isfinite(fs):
        best_score = fs
    print(f"FINAL: internal/safe score={best_score:.6f}, n={len(final_seq)}, time={time.monotonic()-t_global:.0f}s")

    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
